--[[ Butter Hub (Lumber Tycoon 2) - cleaned build
  Removed:
   - Discord webhooks (they sent your Username, UserId, JobId, executor and a join-script to the author)
   - Remote admin chat commands (;kick ;ban ;sh ;rj ... from the author's Roblox group could kick/ban/teleport you)
   - Fake-ban countdown / group-member watcher that pinged the author
   - Remote version check + auto reload of Root2.lua (it fired a Discord auto-invite and loaded other remote code)
   - discord.gg / owner promo strings
  Inlined: ServerHop.txt (now a local, guarded server-hop function)
  Also removed: author-group check, "Did you know" popups. (There was no key system in this script - the "key" is only the UI toggle keybind.)
  Fixed: goto (not valid in Luau) at tree-cut and sort code, "out of local registers" compile error
]]

function switchTab(arg)
    if getService then
        return
    end
    local currentTab = v72.currentTab
    if currentTab == nil then
        arg[2].Visible = true
        v72.currentTab = arg
        theR2.TweenService:Create(arg[1], TweenInfo.new(0.1), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
        theR2.TweenService:Create(arg[1].TabText, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
        return
    end
    if currentTab[1] == arg[1] then
        return
    end
    v72.currentTab = arg
    theR2.TweenService:Create(currentTab[1], TweenInfo.new(0.1), {ImageTransparency = 0.2}):Play()
    theR2.TweenService:Create(arg[1], TweenInfo.new(0.1), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
    theR2.TweenService:Create(currentTab[1].TabText, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextTransparency = 0.2}):Play()
    theR2.TweenService:Create(arg[1].TabText, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
    currentTab[2].Visible = false
    arg[2].Visible = true
    task.wait(0.1)
    getService = false
end
function drag(cframe, cframe5)
    local flag4, input2, position, position2
    if not cframe5 then
        cframe5 = cframe
    end
    flag4 = nil
    input2 = nil
    position = nil
    position2 = nil
    function Ripple(parent2)
        spawn(function()
            if parent2.ClipsDescendants ~= true then
                parent2.ClipsDescendants = true
            end
            local imageLabel3 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            imageLabel3.Name = "Ripple"
            imageLabel3.Parent = parent2
            imageLabel3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            imageLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageLabel3.ZIndex = 8
            imageLabel3.Image = "rbxassetid://2708891598"
            imageLabel3.ImageTransparency = 0.8
            imageLabel3.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            imageLabel3.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            local uDim2 = UDim2.new((player4.X - imageLabel3.AbsolutePosition.X) / parent2.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, (player4.Y - imageLabel3.AbsolutePosition.Y) / parent2.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0)
            imageLabel3.Position = uDim2
            Tween(imageLabel3, {0.3, "Linear", "InOut"}, {
                Position = UDim2.new(-5.5, 0, -5.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(12, 0, 12, 0),
            })
            wait(0.15)
            Tween(imageLabel3, {0.3, "Linear", "InOut"}, {ImageTransparency = 1})
            wait(0.3)
            imageLabel3:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    function update(cframe4)
        local offset = cframe4.Position - position
        cframe.Position = UDim2.new(position2.X.Scale, position2.X.Offset + offset.X, position2.Y.Scale, position2.Y.Offset + offset.Y)
    end
    cframe5.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            flag4 = true
            position = input.Position
            position2 = cframe.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    flag4 = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    cframe.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            input2 = input
        end
    end)
    theR2.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == input2 then
            if flag4 then
                update(input)
            end
        end
    end)
end

str3 = str3(str4, "TweenService")
function str4(arg, arg2)
    local arg6 = arg
    local parent2 = Instance.new(arg6)
    pcall(function()
        parent2.BorderSizePixel = 0
        parent2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        parent2.AutoButtonColor = false
    end)
    for k, v2 in next, arg2, nil do
        parent2[k] = v2
    end
    local tbl3 = {}
    tbl3.object = function(arg3, arg4, instance)
        if not instance.Parent then
            instance.Parent = parent2
        end
        return object(arg4, instance)
    end
    tbl3.round = function(arg3, arg4)
        if not arg4 then
            arg4 = 4
        end
        object("UICorner", {Parent = parent2, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, arg4)})
        return tbl3
    end
    tbl3.tween = function(arg3, arg4)
        str3:Create(parent2, TweenInfo.new(0.25), arg4):Play()
    end
    tbl3.AbsoluteObject = parent2
    return setmetatable(tbl3, {
        __index = function(arg3, arg4)
            return parent2[arg4]

function GetPing()
    local now2 = tick()
    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").TestPing:InvokeServer()
    return (tick() - now2) / 2 + 0.5
end
function GetAutobuildPing()
    local now2 = tick()
    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").TestPing:InvokeServer()
    return ((tick() - now2) / 2 + 0.5) / 10
end
function calculateHitsForEndPart(arg)
    return math.round(math.sqrt(arg.Size.X * arg.Size.Z) ^ 2 * 80000000 / 10000000)
end
function DropTools()
    for _, tool in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
        if tool.Name == "Tool" then
            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(tool, "Drop tool", game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 4, -4))
            wait(0.5)
        end
    end
end
function GetToolsfix()
    for _, playerModel in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants()) do
        if playerModel.Name == "Model" then
            if playerModel:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                if playerModel:FindFirstChild("ToolName") then
                    if playerModel.ToolName.Value == "EndTimesAxe" then
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(playerModel, "Pick up tool")
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function infhrp(arg)
    pcall(function()
        if arg then
            game:GetService("Lighting").HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame

            humanoidRootPartClone.Transparency = 1
            humanoidRootPartClone.Parent = player5.Character
        end
    end)
end

function GetConnection(arg, arg2)
    for k, v2 in next, num11, nil do
        if tostring(k) == tostring(arg) then
            if arg2 then
                v2.Function:Disconnect()
                num11[arg] = nil
                return false
            end
            return v2.Function
        end
    end
    return false
end
function GetBlueprints()
    BluePrints = {}
    for _, child in next, game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").ClientItemInfo:GetChildren() do
        local type2 = child:FindFirstChild("Type")
        do
            local skipped = false
            if type2 then
                type2 = child.Type.Value
                if type2 == "Structure" then
                    skipped = true
                end
            end
            if not skipped then
                type2 = child.Type.Value
            end
        end
        if type2 == "Furniture" then
            if child:FindFirstChild("WoodCost") then
                if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerBlueprints.Blueprints:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
                    table.insert(BluePrints, child.Name)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return BluePrints
end
function ModSawmill()
    if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerBlueprints.Blueprints:FindFirstChild("Floor2") then
        str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "You don't have blueprint Floor2",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
        return
    end
    ModWoodSawmill = nil
    SelectSawmill("To Mod")
    local ModWoodSawmill2
    repeat
        Wait()
        ModWoodSawmill2 = ModWoodSawmill
    until ModWoodSawmill2
    local v27 = nil
    local children = ModWoodSawmill.Conveyor.Model:GetChildren()
    local y = ModWoodSawmill.Main.Orientation.Y
    local v28 = ModWoodSawmill.ItemName.Value:match("Sawmill4L")
    do
        local skipped = false
        if v28 then
            v28 = #children - 1
            if v28 then
                skipped = true
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            v28 = #children
        end
    end
    for i = v28, #children do
        v27 = children[i]
        break
    end
    local num28 = 0.4
    local v2, v3
    for i = 1, 4 do
        num28 = num28 + 0.2
        local remote = num10
        local p = v27.CFrame.p
        if y == 0 then
            v2 = -num28
        end
        local v29 = num28 or v2
        if not v29 and (y ~= 180 or not num28) then
            v29 = 0
        end
        if y == -90 then
            v3 = -num28
        end
        local cFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(v29, 1.5, v3 or y == 90 and v3))
        local num29 = math.rad((y == 180 or y == 0) and 90 or 45)
        v2 = math.rad((y == 180 or y == 0) and 0 or 90)
        local v30
        v30, v3 = math.rad((y == 180 or y == 0) and 90 or 45)
        remote:FireServer("Floor2", cFrame * CFrame.Angles(num29, v2, v30, v3), player5)
        Wait(1.5)
    end
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = "Fill Blueprints To Complete.",
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
    ModWoodSawmill = nil
end
b2 = nil
function getService2(arg)
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = string.format("Select A Sawmill To %s.", arg),
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
    if b2 then
        b2:Disconnect()
    end
    local connection = player6.Button1Down:Connect(function()
        local target2 = player6.Target
        if not target2 then
            return
        end
        local parent2 = target2.Parent
        local settings = parent2:FindFirstChild("Settings")
        do
            local skipped = false
            if settings then
                settings = parent2.Settings:FindFirstChild("DimZ")
                if settings then
                    skipped = true
                end
            end
            if not skipped then
                settings = parent2.Parent:FindFirstChild("Settings")
                if settings then
                    settings = parent2.Parent.Settings:FindFirstChild("DimZ")
                end
            end
        end
        if settings then
            ModWoodSawmill = settings.Parent.Parent
            str5:message({
                Title = "Butter",
                Description = "Sawmill Selected.",
                Icon = 6023426926,
            })
            b2:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
    b2 = connection
end

function getService2(arg)
    local callback17, x
    SelectSawmill("Mod Wood")
    local visible
    repeat
        local ModWoodSawmill2
        repeat
            Wait()
            ModWoodSawmill2 = ModWoodSawmill
        until ModWoodSawmill2
        visible = callback5.NoticeGUI.Notice.Visible
    until not visible
    local value = ClientItemInfo:FindFirstChild(ModWoodSawmill.ItemName.Value).OtherInfo.MaxOutputSize.Value
    callback17 = "%.1f"
    x = value.X
    local v2, v3, v29, v32
    v2, callback17, x, v3, v29, v32 = string.format(callback17, x)
    local num28 = tonumber(v2, callback17, x, v3, v29, v32)
    callback17 = string
    callback17 = callback17.format
    x = "%.1f"
    local v27, v30, v33
    callback17, x, v27, v30, v33 = callback17(x, value.Z)
    local num29 = tonumber(callback17, x, v27, v30, v33)
    callback17 = num28 or callback17
    if arg ~= "Maximum" or not num28 then
        callback17 = tonumber
        x = string
        x = x.format
        local v28, v31, v76
        x, v28, v31, v76 = x("%.1f", 0.6)
        callback17 = callback17(x, v28, v31, v76)
    end
    x = num29 or x
    if arg ~= "Maximum" or not num29 then
        x = tonumber
        x = x(string.format("%.1f", 0.4))
    end
    Maid.Threads:Create(function()
        local num30, v28
        repeat
            Wait()
            RemoteProxy:FireServer(ModWoodSawmill:FindFirstChild(arg == "Maximum" and "ButtonRemote_XUp" or "ButtonRemote_XDown"))
            num30 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", ModWoodSawmill.Settings.DimX.Value))
            v28 = callback17
        until num30 == v28
    end)
    Maid.Threads:Create(function()
        local num30, x3
        repeat
            Wait()
            RemoteProxy:FireServer(ModWoodSawmill:FindFirstChild(arg == "Maximum" and "ButtonRemote_YUp" or "ButtonRemote_YDown"))
            num30 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", ModWoodSawmill.Settings.DimZ.Value))
            x3 = x
        until num30 == x3
    end)
end

function getService2(arg, arg2)
    for _, child in next, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetChildren() do
        if child.Name ~= "Chat" then
            if child.Name ~= "TargetGui" then
                if child.Name ~= "LoadingScreen" then
                    if child.Name ~= "Blueprints" then
                        for _, descendant in next, child:GetDescendants() do
                            if not descendant:FindFirstChild("UICorner") then
                                Instance.new("UICorner", descendant)
                            end
                            if descendant.Name == "DropShadow" then
                                descendant:Destroy()
                            end
                            local v2 = descendant:IsA("TextButton")
                            if v2 or descendant:IsA("Frame") or descendant:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                                descendant.BackgroundColor3 = arg
                            end
                            local v3 = descendant:IsA("TextLabel")
                            if v3 or descendant:IsA("TextButton") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                                descendant.TextColor3 = arg2
                                descendant.BackgroundColor3 = arg
                                descendant.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function getService2(cframe)
    if not firetouchinterest then
        str5:message({
            Title = "Executor issue",
            Description = "Does not have firetouchinterest!",
            Icon = 6023426926,
            Length = 1,
        })
        return
    end
    local lava = GetLava()
    player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1439.45, 433.4, 1317.61)
    local lavaFire
    repeat
        task.wait(1)
        if identifyexecutor() == "Wave" then
            firetouchinterest(player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart, lava.Lava, true)
        else
            firetouchinterest(player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart, lava.Lava, 0)
        end
        lavaFire = player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("LavaFire")
    until lavaFire
    player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.LavaFire:Destroy()
    task.wait(1)
    local torsoClone = player5.Character.Torso:Clone()
    torsoClone.Name = "HumanoidRootPart"
    torsoClone.Transparency = 1
    torsoClone.Parent = player5.Character
    player5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe
    torsoClone.CFrame = cframe
end

function num12(arg, arg2)

        local cframe = arg or v2

        end

            game:GetService("Lighting").HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe

            game:GetService("Lighting").HumanoidRootPart.Vector3 = Vector3.new(cframe)

        end
    end
end

getToolStats = num12
function num12(arg)
    local v27 = getTools()
    if #v27 == 0 then
        return str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "Need Axe",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
    end
    local list = {}
    local v28 = nil
    for _, v2 in next, v27, nil do
        if arg == "LoneCave" then
            if v2.ToolName.Value == "EndTimesAxe" then
                v28 = v2
                break
            end
        end
        local v30 = getToolStats(v2)
        if v30.SpecialTrees then
            if v30.SpecialTrees[arg] then
                for k, v3 in next, v30.SpecialTrees[arg], nil do
                    v30[k] = v3
                end
            end
        end
        table.insert(list, {tool = v2, damage = v30.Damage})
    end
    if not v28 and arg == "LoneCave" then
        return str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "Need Endtimes Axe",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
    end
    local list6 = list
    table.sort(list6, function(arg2, arg3)
        return arg2.damage > arg3.damage
    end)
    local list7 = v28 or list6
    if not v28 then
        list7 = list[1].tool
    end
    return true, list7
end
getBestAxe = num12
function num12(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
    local v3 = getToolStats(arg4)
    if v3.SpecialTrees then
        if v3.SpecialTrees[arg5] then
            for k, v2 in next, v3.SpecialTrees[arg5], nil do
                v3[k] = v2
            end
        end
    end
    local remoteProxy = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.RemoteProxy
    local tbl3 = {tool = arg4}
    local vector3 = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)
    tbl3.faceVector = vector3
    local vector = arg3 or vector3
    if not arg3 then
        vector = 0.3
    end
    tbl3.height = vector
    local v28 = arg2 or vector
    if not arg2 then
        v28 = 1
    end
    tbl3.sectionId = v28
    tbl3.hitPoints = v3.Damage
    tbl3.cooldown = v3.SwingCooldown
    tbl3.cuttingClass = "Axe"
    remoteProxy:FireServer(arg, tbl3)
end

function str10()
    while true do
        if not task.wait() then
            break
        end
        for _, child in next, workspace:GetChildren() do
            if child.Name == "TreeRegion" then
                num13[child] = {}
                for _, child2 in next, child:GetChildren() do
                    if child2:FindFirstChild("TreeClass") then
                        if not table.find(list4, child2.TreeClass.Value) then
                            table.insert(list4, child2.TreeClass.Value)
                        end
                    end
                    if child2:FindFirstChild("TreeClass") then
                        if not table.find(num13[child], child2.TreeClass.Value) then
                            table.insert(num13[child], child2.TreeClass.Value)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
v17(str10)
function v17(arg)
    local list6 = {}
    for k, list in next, num13, nil do
        if table.find(list, arg) then
            for _, model in next, k:GetChildren() do
                if model:IsA("Model") then
                    if model:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                        local model2 = model
                        local firstChild = model.FindFirstChild(model2, "TreeClass")
                        do
                            local skipped = false
                            if firstChild then
                                firstChild = model.TreeClass.Value
                                if firstChild == arg then
                                    firstChild = model.Owner.Value
                                    if firstChild == nil then
                                        skipped = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if not skipped then
                                firstChild = model.Owner.Value
                                model2 = player7
                            end
                        end
                        if firstChild == model2 then
                            local num28 = 0
                            local basePart3 = nil
                            for _, basePart in next, model:GetChildren() do
                                if basePart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    if basePart:FindFirstChild("ID") then
                                        if basePart.ID.Value == 1 then
                                            basePart3 = basePart
                                        end
                                    end
                                    num28 = num28 + basePart:GetMass()
                                end
                            end
                            table.insert(list6, {tree = model, trunk = basePart3, mass = num28})
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(list6, function(arg2, arg3)
        return arg2.mass > arg3.mass
    end)
    local v2 = list6[1]
    if not v2 then
        v2 = nil
    end
    return v2
end

function bringTree(arg, arg2)
    getgenv2.treestop = true
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
    local localPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local v3, v27 = getBestAxe(arg)
    local getToolStats2 = getToolStats
    local v2 = v27
    local v28 = getToolStats2(v2)
    local callback18 = v17
    local pos2 = arg
    local v32 = callback18(pos2)
    if not v32 then
        return
    end
    pos2 = v32.trunk
    pos2 = pos2.Size
    pos2 = pos2.X
    do
        local skipped = false
        if 1 <= pos2 then
            pos2 = v32.trunk
            pos2 = pos2.Size
            pos2 = pos2.Y
            if 2 <= pos2 then
                pos2 = v32.trunk
                pos2 = pos2.Size
                pos2 = pos2.Z
                if 1 <= pos2 then
                    skipped = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            pos2 = print
            return pos2("Too Small")
        end
    end
    pos2 = getPosition
    pos2 = pos2()
    if arg2 then
        if firetouchinterest then
            Workspace.Camera.CameraType = "Fixed"
            GodMode(v32.trunk.CFrame)
            Workspace.Camera.CameraType = "Custom"
            tp(v32.trunk.CFrame)
        else
            str5:message({
                Title = "Executor issue",
                Description = "Does not have firetouchinterest!",
                Icon = 6023426926,
                Length = 1,
            })
        end
    end
    wait(0.5)
    if getgenv2.Infeaxerange and not arg2 then
        infhrp()
    end
    num12(arg, function(instance)
        instance.PrimaryPart = instance:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
        getgenv2.treeCut = true
        for i = 1, 60 do
            DragModel1(instance, pos2)
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
    task.wait(0.15)
    task.spawn(function()
        if arg == "LoneCave" then
            getgenv2.doneend = false
            if not getgenv2.treestop then
                return
            end
            tp(v32.trunk.CFrame)
            task.wait()
            if getgenv2.treeCut then
                return
            end
        end
        local treeCut
        repeat
            if not getgenv2.treestop then
                break
            end
            tp(v32.trunk.CFrame, getgenv2.Infeaxerange)
            task.wait()
            treeCut = getgenv2.treeCut
        until treeCut
    end)
    task.wait()
    if arg == "LoneCave" and arg2 then
        numberofhits = calculateHitsForEndPart(v32.trunk)
        numberofhits = numberofhits - 1
        print(numberofhits)
        for i = 1, numberofhits do
            cutPart(v32.tree.CutEvent, 1, 0.3, v27, arg)
            task.wait(1)
        end
        getgenv2.treeCut = false
        getgenv2.treestop = false
        DropTools()
        wait(0.3)
        workspace[str10].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1675, 261, 1284)
        wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            local health
            repeat
                wait()
                health = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health
            until health == 100
        end)
        wait(0.3)
        GetToolsfix()
        wait(0.5)
        bringTree("LoneCave", false)
    else
        local treeCut
        repeat
            if not getgenv2.treestop then
                break
            end
            cutPart(v32.tree.CutEvent, 1, 0.3, v27, arg)
            task.wait()
            treeCut = getgenv2.treeCut
        until treeCut
    end
    if arg == "LoneCave" then
        wait(1)
        tp(pos2)
        getgenv2.doneend = true
        getgenv2.treeCut = false
        getgenv2.treestop = false
    elseif getgenv2.Infeaxerange and not arg2 then
        getgenv2.treeCut = false
        infhrp(true)
    else
        wait(1)
        getgenv2.treeCut = false
        tp(pos2)
    end
end

function tweenService5(character)
    if character:WaitForChild("Owner") then
        if character.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
            if character:WaitForChild("TreeClass") then
                if character.TreeClass.Value == Type then
                    if character:WaitForChild("WoodSection") then
                        if not character.PrimaryPart then
                            character.PrimaryPart = character:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
                        end
                        for i = 1, 50 do
                            game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(character)
                            character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cframe2)
                            task.wait()
                            getgenv2.EndTree = true
                        end
                        flag5 = true
                        if Type == "LoneCave" then
                            getgenv2.treeCut = false
                            getgenv2.treestop = false
                            getgenv2.doneend = true
                            getgenv2.SetSwing = false
                            getgenv2.EndTree = true
                            if AxeAdded then
                                AxeAdded:Disconnect()
                            end
                            wait(10)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function tweenService5(instance)
    local humanoidRootPart = instance:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart then
        humanoidRootPart = instance:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            humanoidRootPart = instance:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        end
    end
    return humanoidRootPart

FLYING = false

function tweenService5(arg)
    local wait2, player2, str14, num28
    repeat
        repeat
            repeat
                repeat
                    wait()
                    wait2 = callback4
                    wait2 = wait2.LocalPlayer
                until wait2
                wait2 = callback4

                wait2 = wait2.Character
            until wait2

            player2 = player2.Character
            wait2 = wait2(player2)
        until wait2
        wait2 = callback4
        wait2 = wait2.LocalPlayer
        wait2 = wait2.Character
        player2 = wait2
        wait2 = wait2.FindFirstChildOfClass
        str14 = "Humanoid"
        wait2 = wait2(player2, str14)
    until wait2
    repeat
        wait()
        wait2 = IYMouse
    until wait2
    wait2 = flyKeyDown
    do
        local skipped = false
        if not wait2 then
            wait2 = flyKeyUp
            if not wait2 then
                skipped = true
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            wait2 = flyKeyDown
            player2 = wait2
            wait2 = wait2.Disconnect
            wait2(player2)
            wait2 = flyKeyUp
            player2 = wait2
            wait2 = wait2.Disconnect
            wait2(player2)
        end
    end
    wait2 = getRoot
    player2 = callback4
    player2 = player2.LocalPlayer
    player2 = player2.Character
    wait2 = wait2(player2)
    player2 = {}
    player2.F = 0
    player2.B = 0
    player2.L = 0
    player2.R = 0
    player2.Q = 0
    player2.E = 0
    str14 = {}
    str14.F = 0
    str14.B = 0
    str14.L = 0
    str14.R = 0
    str14.Q = 0
    str14.E = 0
    num28 = 0
    function FLY()
        FLYING = true
        local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bodyGyro.P = 90000
        bodyGyro.Parent = wait2
        bodyVelocity.Parent = wait2
        bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
        bodyGyro.cframe = wait2.CFrame
        bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        bodyVelocity.maxForce = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
        task.spawn(function()
            local FLYING2
            repeat
                wait()
                if not arg then
                    if callback4.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                        local humanoid2 = callback4.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        humanoid2.PlatformStand = true
                    end
                end
                local v2 = player2.L + player2.R
                do
                    local skipped = false
                    if v2 ~= 0 or player2.F + player2.B ~= 0 or player2.Q + player2.E ~= 0 then
                        num28 = 50
                        skipped = true
                    end
                    if not skipped then
                        if player2.L + player2.R == 0 then
                            if player2.F + player2.B == 0 then
                                if player2.Q + player2.E == 0 then
                                    if num28 ~= 0 then
                                        num28 = 0
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local v3 = player2.L + player2.R
                do
                    local skipped = false
                    if v3 ~= 0 or player2.F + player2.B ~= 0 or player2.Q + player2.E ~= 0 then
                        local v28 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (player2.F + player2.B)
                        local v29 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(player2.L + player2.R, (player2.F + player2.B + player2.Q + player2.E) * 0.2, 0).p - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p
                        bodyVelocity.velocity = (v28 + v29) * num28
                        str14 = {F = player2.F, B = player2.B, L = player2.L, R = player2.R}
                        skipped = true
                    end
                    if not skipped then
                        if player2.L + player2.R == 0 then
                            if player2.F + player2.B == 0 then
                                if player2.Q + player2.E == 0 then
                                    if num28 ~= 0 then
                                        local v28 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (str14.F + str14.B)
                                        local v29 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(str14.L + str14.R, (str14.F + str14.B + player2.Q + player2.E) * 0.2, 0).p - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p
                                        bodyVelocity.velocity = (v28 + v29) * num28
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
                bodyGyro.cframe = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
                FLYING2 = FLYING
            until not FLYING2
            player2 = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            str14 = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            num28 = 0
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            if callback4.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                local humanoid2 = callback4.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                humanoid2.PlatformStand = false
            end
        end)
    end
    local connection = IYMouse.KeyDown:Connect(function(arg2)
        if arg2:lower() == "w" then
            player2.F = arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed
        elseif arg2:lower() == "s" then
            player2.B = -(arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif arg2:lower() == "a" then
            player2.L = -(arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif arg2:lower() == "d" then
            player2.R = arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed
        elseif QEfly then
            if arg2:lower() == "e" then
                player2.Q = (arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 2
            end
        elseif QEfly then
            if arg2:lower() == "q" then
                player2.E = -(arg and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 2
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Track
        end)
    end)
    flyKeyDown = connection
    local connection12 = IYMouse.KeyUp:Connect(function(arg2)
        if arg2:lower() == "w" then
            player2.F = 0
        elseif arg2:lower() == "s" then
            player2.B = 0
        elseif arg2:lower() == "a" then
            player2.L = 0
        elseif arg2:lower() == "d" then
            player2.R = 0
        elseif arg2:lower() == "e" then
            player2.Q = 0
        elseif arg2:lower() == "q" then
            player2.E = 0
        end
    end)
    flyKeyUp = connection12
    FLY()
end

function tweenService5()
    game.Lighting:ClearAllChildren()
    local colorCorrectionEffect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", game.Lighting)
    local bloomEffect = Instance.new("BloomEffect", game.Lighting)
    local sunRaysEffect = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect", game.Lighting)
    local blurEffect = Instance.new("BlurEffect", game.Lighting)
    colorCorrectionEffect.Enabled = true
    colorCorrectionEffect.Contrast = 0.15
    colorCorrectionEffect.Brightness = 0.1
    colorCorrectionEffect.Saturation = 0.25
    colorCorrectionEffect.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 222, 211)
    sunRaysEffect.Enabled = true
    sunRaysEffect.Intensity = 0.2
    sunRaysEffect.Spread = 1
    bloomEffect.Enabled = true
    bloomEffect.Intensity = 1
    bloomEffect.Size = 32
    bloomEffect.Threshold = 1
    blurEffect.Enabled = true
    blurEffect.Size = 3
    game.Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    game.Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    game.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    game.Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
    game.Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
    game.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(112, 117, 128)
    game.Lighting.Outlines = false
end

function tweenService5(arg)
    for _, store in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Stores:GetChildren()) do
        if store.Name == "ShopItems" then
            for _, child in pairs(store:GetChildren()) do
                if child:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                    if child.Owner.Value == nil then
                        if child:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName") then
                            if tostring(child.BoxItemName.Value) == arg then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

GetPrice = tweenService5
function tweenService5()
    local list = {}
    for _, store in next, game:GetService("Workspace").Stores:GetChildren() do
        if store.Name == "ShopItems" then
            for _, child in next, store:GetChildren() do
                if child:FindFirstChild("Type") then
                    if child.Type.Value ~= "Blueprint" then
                        if child:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName") then
                            if not table.find(list, child.BoxItemName.Value .. " - $" .. GetPrice(child.BoxItemName.Value, 1)) then
                                table.insert(list, child.BoxItemName.Value .. " - $" .. GetPrice(child.BoxItemName.Value, 1))
                                task.wait(0.01)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(list)
    end
    return list
end

        tick2 = str5

        tick2 = tick2.message

            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "No money",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
    end

            break

            game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(instance)
            task.wait()
            value = instance.Owner.Value
        until value ~= nil
        if instance.Owner.Value ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
            break
        end

                if instance.Owner.Value ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                    break
                end

            Pay(tbl[instance4.Parent.Name])

            ResizeBar(i, arg2)

function num14()
    for _, playerModel in next, game:GetService("Workspace").PlayerModels:GetChildren() do
        if playerModel:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if playerModel.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                if playerModel:FindFirstChild("ItemName") then
                    if playerModel.ItemName.Value == "PropertySoldSign" then
                        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(playerModel.Main.CFrame.p) + Vector3.new(0, 0, 2)
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(playerModel, "Take down sold sign")
                        for i = 1, 30 do
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(playerModel)
                            playerModel.Main.CFrame = CFrame.new(314.54, -0.5, 86.823)
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function BetterFly()
    local player2, character, flag4, tbl3, tbl4, num28
    local v3
    repeat
        local v2
        repeat
            local character2
            repeat
                local localPlayer2
                repeat
                    wait()
                    localPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                until localPlayer2
                character2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            until character2
            player2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            character = "Head"
            v2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.findFirstChild(player2, character)
        until v2
        player2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        character = "Humanoid"
        v3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.findFirstChild(player2, character)
    until v3
    player2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local mouse = game.Players.LocalPlayer.GetMouse(player2)
    repeat
        player2 = wait
        player2()
    until mouse
    player2 = game
    player2 = player2.Players
    player2 = player2.LocalPlayer
    character = player2.Character
    character = character.Head
    flag4 = false
    tbl3 = {}
    tbl3.f = 0
    tbl3.b = 0
    tbl3.l = 0
    tbl3.r = 0
    tbl4 = {}
    tbl4.f = 0
    tbl4.b = 0
    tbl4.l = 0
    tbl4.r = 0
    num28 = 5000
    function Fly()
        local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", character)
        bodyGyro.P = 90000
        bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
        bodyGyro.cframe = character.CFrame
        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", character)
        bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
        bodyVelocity.maxForce = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
        local flag7
        repeat
            wait()
            player2.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
            local v2 = tbl3.l + tbl3.r
            do
                local skipped = false
                if v2 ~= 0 or tbl3.f + tbl3.b ~= 0 then
                    num28 = callback7
                    skipped = true
                end
                if not skipped then
                    if tbl3.l + tbl3.r == 0 then
                        if tbl3.f + tbl3.b == 0 then
                            if num28 ~= 0 then
                                num28 = 0
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local v27 = tbl3.l + tbl3.r
            do
                local skipped = false
                if v27 ~= 0 or tbl3.f + tbl3.b ~= 0 then
                    local v28 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (tbl3.f + tbl3.b)
                    local v30 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(tbl3.l + tbl3.r, (tbl3.f + tbl3.b) * 0.2, 0).p - game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p
                    bodyVelocity.velocity = (v28 + v30) * num28
                    tbl4 = {f = tbl3.f, b = tbl3.b, l = tbl3.l, r = tbl3.r}
                    skipped = true
                end
                if not skipped then
                    if tbl3.l + tbl3.r == 0 then
                        if tbl3.f + tbl3.b == 0 then
                            if num28 ~= 0 then
                                local v28 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (tbl4.f + tbl4.b)
                                local v30 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(tbl4.l + tbl4.r, (tbl4.f + tbl4.b) * 0.2, 0).p - game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p
                                bodyVelocity.velocity = (v28 + v30) * num28
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
            bodyGyro.cframe = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.Angles(-math.rad((tbl3.f + tbl3.b) * num28 / callback7), 0, 0)
            flag7 = flag4
        until not flag7
        tbl3 = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0}
        tbl4 = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0}
        num28 = 0
        bodyGyro:Destroy()
        bodyVelocity:Destroy()
        player2.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
    end
    mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(arg)
        if arg:lower() == num14 then
            if b3 == true then
                if flag4 then
                    flag4 = false
                else
                    flag4 = true
                    Fly()
                end
            end
        elseif arg:lower() == "w" then
            tbl3.f = 1
        elseif arg:lower() == "s" then
            tbl3.b = -1
        elseif arg:lower() == "a" then
            tbl3.l = -1
        elseif arg:lower() == "d" then
            tbl3.r = 1
        end
    end)
    mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(arg)
        if arg:lower() == "w" then
            tbl3.f = 0
        elseif arg:lower() == "s" then
            tbl3.b = 0
        elseif arg:lower() == "a" then
            tbl3.l = 0
        elseif arg:lower() == "d" then
            tbl3.r = 0
        end
    end)
    Fly()
end

        table.remove(parentfix, #parentfix)
    end)

function carTP(cframe)
    player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    humanoid = player.Character.Humanoid
    if humanoid.Seated then
        local seatPart = player.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart
        if seatPart then
            if seatPart.Parent.Type.Value == "Vehicle" then
                seatPart.CFrame = cframe
                seatPart.Parent.RightSteer.Wheel.CFrame = cframe
                seatPart.Parent.LeftSteer.Wheel.CFrame = cframe
                seatPart.Parent.RightPower.Wheel.CFrame = cframe
                seatPart.Parent.LeftPower.Wheel.CFrame = cframe
            end
        end
    end
end

function isValidModel(instance)

    if main2 then

        local match4 = string.find(v2, "pickup")
        local match5 = string.find(v2, "PropertySoldSign")
        main2 = not match5 and match5
    end
    return main2
end

function lassoTpcheck()
    function is_in_frame(pos2, arg)
        local x = arg.AbsolutePosition.X
        local y = arg.AbsolutePosition.Y
        local x2 = arg.AbsoluteSize.X
        local y2 = arg.AbsoluteSize.Y
        local isMatch = x <= pos2.X
        local isMatch2 = x >= pos2.X
        local isMatch3 = y <= pos2.Y
        local isMatch4 = y >= pos2.Y
        local v2
        local isMatch5 = isMatch2 or (isMatch4 or (isMatch3 or (isMatch3 or v2)))
        if (not isMatch or not isMatch3) and (not isMatch2 or not isMatch3) and (not isMatch or not isMatch4) and isMatch2 then
            isMatch5 = isMatch4
        end
        return isMatch5
    end
end

function BringAllLogs()
    cframe2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
    for _, logModel in next, game:GetService("Workspace").LogModels:GetChildren() do
        if logModel:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if logModel.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(logModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection").CFrame.p)
                if not logModel.PrimaryPart then
                    logModel.PrimaryPart = logModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
                end
                for i = 1, 50 do
                    game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(logModel)
                    logModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cframe2)
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait()
    end
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe2
end
function SellAllLogs()
    cframe2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
    for _, logModel in next, game:GetService("Workspace").LogModels:GetChildren() do
        if logModel:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if logModel.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(logModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection").CFrame.p)
                task.wait(0.3)

                    logModel.PrimaryPart = logModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")

                spawn(function()
                    for i = 1, 50 do
                        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(logModel)
                        logModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(314, -0.5, 86.822))
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        task.wait()
    end
    task.wait()
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe2
end

treeCutNoRequire = num18
function num18(arg)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Tool") then
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:UnequipTools()
    end
    local tool4 = nil
    for _, tool in next, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren() do
        if tool.Name == "Tool" then
            local module = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. tool.ToolName.Value))
            if 0 < module.new().Damage then
                tool4 = tool
                local module2 = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. tool.ToolName.Value))
                if module2.new().SpecialTrees then
                    local module3 = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. tool.ToolName.Value))
                    if module3.new().SpecialTrees[arg] then
                        return tool
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return tool4
end
GetBestAxe = num18
function num18(arg)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Tool") then
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:UnequipTools()
    end
    local module = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. GetBestAxe(arg).ToolName.Value))
    if module.new().SpecialTrees then
        local module2 = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. GetBestAxe(arg).ToolName.Value))
        if module2.new().SpecialTrees[arg] then
            local module3 = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. GetBestAxe(arg).ToolName.Value))
            return module3.new().SpecialTrees[arg].Damage
        end
    end
    local module4 = require(v21:FindFirstChild("AxeClass_" .. GetBestAxe(arg).ToolName.Value))
    return module4.new().Damage
end

ChopTree = num18
function num18(arg)
    local player2
    TreeToJointCut = player2
    player2 = game
    player2 = player2.Players
    player2 = player2.LocalPlayer
    player2 = player2.Character
    player2 = player2.HumanoidRootPart
    player2 = player2.CFrame
    cframe2 = player2
    player2 = false
    local connection = game:GetService("Workspace").LogModels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:WaitForChild("Owner") then
            if child.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                if child:WaitForChild("WoodSection") then
                    player2 = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    branchadded = connection
    if not arg then
        local connection12 = pos.Button1Up:Connect(function()
            Clicked = pos.Target
            if Clicked.Parent:FindFirstAncestor("LogModels") then
                if Clicked.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                    if Clicked.Parent.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                        TreeToJointCut = Clicked.Parent
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        DismemberTreeC = connection12
    else
        TreeToJointCut = arg
    end
    local str14
    repeat
        task.wait()
        str14 = tostring(TreeToJointCut)
    until str14 ~= "nil"
    if TreeToJointCut:FindFirstChild("WoodClass") then
        if TreeToJointCut.WoodClass.Value == "LoneCave" then
            if not getBestAxeNoRequire("EndTimesAxe") then
                return print("Error", "You need an end times axe")
            end
        end
    end
    for _, child in next, TreeToJointCut:GetChildren() do
        if child.Name == "WoodSection" then
            if child:FindFirstChild("ID") then
                if child.ID.Value ~= 1 then
                    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(child.CFrame.p)
                    repeat
                        ChopTree(child.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent"), child.ID.Value, 0, child.Parent:FindFirstChild("TreeClass").Value)
                        task.wait()
                    until player2 == true
                    player2 = false
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    branchadded:Disconnect()
    DismemberTreeC:Disconnect()
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe2
    return TreeToJointCut
end

function updateOrbit()
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
        local orbitItem = getgenv2.orbitItem
        local primaryPart = getgenv2.orbitItem.PrimaryPart
        do
            local skipped = false
            if not primaryPart then
                primaryPart = getgenv2.orbitItem:FindFirstChild("Main")
                if primaryPart then
                    primaryPart = getgenv2.orbitItem.Main
                    if primaryPart then
                        skipped = true
                    end
                end
                if not skipped then
                    primaryPart = getgenv2.orbitItem:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
                    if primaryPart then
                        primaryPart = getgenv2.orbitItem.WoodSection
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        orbitItem.PrimaryPart = primaryPart
        local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local v2 = tick() * orbitSpeed
        local vector3 = Vector3.new(orbitDistance * math.cos(v2), 0, orbitDistance * math.sin(v2))
        local vector32 = Vector3.new(orbitDistance * math.cos(v2 + math.pi), 0, orbitDistance * math.sin(v2 + math.pi))
        getgenv2.orbitItem.PrimaryPart.Position = humanoidRootPart.Position + vector3
    end
end
function KillPlayer()
    if not game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart then
        return str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "You need to sit in a car for this feature!",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
    end
    track4 = function(arg)
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(arg, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
    end
    local seatPart
    repeat
        wait()
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(game.Players[plrselected].Character.Head.CFrame, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(180), 0, 0))
        seatPart = game.Players[plrselected].Character.Humanoid.SeatPart
    until seatPart
    track4(CFrame.new(-1675.2, 261.303, 1284.2))
end

            str15 = "Selection"

                num30 = -3

                findFirstChild(instance7, str15)

                            findFirstChild = SelTree

                break
            end
            twes(callback10, {Rotation = callback10.Rotation + 360}, "Linear", "In", 6)
            wait(6)
        end
        game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(callback10, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), changes):Play()
    end)
end
function delallselections()
    for _, playerModel in pairs(game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        if playerModel:FindFirstChild("Main") then
            if playerModel.Main:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                playerModel.Main.Selection:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    for _, playerModel in pairs(game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        if playerModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
            if playerModel.WoodSection:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                playerModel.WoodSection.Selection:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    for _, playerModel in pairs(game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        if playerModel:FindFirstChild("BuildDependentWood") then
            if playerModel.BuildDependentWood:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                playerModel.BuildDependentWood.Selection:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end
function truckTP()
    local player2, player14, tbl3, findFirstChild
    player2 = game
    player2 = player2.Players
    player2 = player2.LocalPlayer
    player2 = player2.Character
    player2 = player2.Humanoid
    player2 = player2.SeatPart
    if not player2 then
        player2 = str5
        player14 = player2
        player2 = player2.message
        tbl3 = {}
        tbl3.Title = "Butter"
        tbl3.Description = "You need to sit in a vehicle to use this feature"
        tbl3.Icon = 6023426926
        player2(player14, tbl3)
        return
    end
    player2 = workspace
    player14 = player2
    player2 = player2.FindFirstChild
    tbl3 = "Cords"
    player2 = player2(player14, tbl3)
    if not player2 then
        player2 = str5
        player14 = player2
        player2 = player2.message
        tbl3 = {}
        tbl3.Title = "Butter"
        tbl3.Description = "You need to set coords to use this feature"
        tbl3.Icon = 6023426926
        player2(player14, tbl3)
        return
    end
    player2 = {}
    player14 = {}
    function tbl3(arg)
        local findFirstChild2
        for _, playerModel in Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren() do
            while true do
                findFirstChild2 = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(playerModel, "Owner")
                if not findFirstChild2 then
                    break
                end
                findFirstChild2 = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(playerModel, "Main")
                if not findFirstChild2 then
                    findFirstChild2 = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                    findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(playerModel, "WoodSection")
                    if not findFirstChild2 then
                        findFirstChild2 = playerModel.PrimaryPart
                        if not findFirstChild2 then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                findFirstChild2 = table
                findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2.find
                findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(player2, playerModel)
                if not findFirstChild2 then
                    findFirstChild2 = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                    findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(playerModel, "Main")
                    if not findFirstChild2 then
                        findFirstChild2 = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                        findFirstChild2 = findFirstChild2(playerModel, "WoodSection")
                        if not findFirstChild2 then
                            findFirstChild2 = playerModel.PrimaryPart
                        end
                    end
                    if isnetworkowner(findFirstChild2) then
                        if playerModel.Owner.Value ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                            break
                        end
                        if not arg then
                            if findFirstChild2:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                                player14[findFirstChild2] = findFirstChild2.CFrame
                                findFirstChild2.CFrame = workspace.Cords.CFrame
                                table.insert(player2, playerModel)
                                pcall(function()
                                    findFirstChild2:FindFirstChild("Selection"):Destroy()
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                break
            end
        end
    end
    BringAllOwnedParts = tbl3
    function tbl3(arg)
        local now2 = tick()
        while true do
            if isnetworkowner(arg) then
                break
            end
            if not (tick() - now2 < 5) then
                break
            end
            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(arg.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 10) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(180), 0, 0))
            wait(0.01)
        end
        BringAllOwnedParts()
        if tick() - now2 < 5 then
            if not isnetworkowner(arg) then
                arg.CFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 10)
            end
        end
    end
    getOwnership = tbl3
    tbl3 = {}
    function vnoclip(arg)
        if arg then
            for _, basePart in game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:GetDescendants() do
                if basePart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if basePart.CanCollide then
                        table.insert(tbl3, basePart)
                        basePart.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            for _, v2 in tbl3, nil, nil do
                v2.CanCollide = true
            end
            tbl3 = {}
        end
    end
    vnoclip(true)
    for _, playerModel in Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren() do
        while true do
            findFirstChild = playerModel.FindFirstChild
            findFirstChild = findFirstChild(playerModel, "Owner")
            if not findFirstChild then
                break
            end
            findFirstChild = playerModel.FindFirstChild
            findFirstChild = findFirstChild(playerModel, "Main")
            if not findFirstChild then
                findFirstChild = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                findFirstChild = findFirstChild(playerModel, "WoodSection")
                if not findFirstChild then
                    findFirstChild = playerModel.PrimaryPart
                    if not findFirstChild then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            findFirstChild = playerModel.FindFirstChild
            findFirstChild = findFirstChild(playerModel, "Main")
            if not findFirstChild then
                findFirstChild = playerModel.FindFirstChild
                findFirstChild = findFirstChild(playerModel, "WoodSection")
                if not findFirstChild then
                    findFirstChild = playerModel.PrimaryPart
                end
            end
            local selection = findFirstChild:FindFirstChild("Selection")
            if selection or playerModel.Owner.Value ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                player14[findFirstChild] = findFirstChild.CFrame
                getOwnership(findFirstChild)
                BringAllOwnedParts()
                findFirstChild.CFrame = workspace.Cords.CFrame
                table.insert(player2, playerModel)
                pcall(function()
                    findFirstChild:FindFirstChild("Selection"):Destroy()
                end)
            end
            break
        end
    end
    wait(3)
    for k, v2 in player14, nil, nil do
        findFirstChild = player2
        for k2, instance in findFirstChild, nil, nil do
            local main2 = instance:FindFirstChild("Main")
            if k == main2 or k == instance:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                player2[k2] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    for k, cframe in player14, nil, nil do
        findFirstChild = k.CFrame
        findFirstChild = findFirstChild.Position
        findFirstChild = findFirstChild - cframe.Position
        findFirstChild = findFirstChild.Magnitude
        if findFirstChild <= 5 then
            getOwnership(k)
            BringAllOwnedParts(true)
            k.CFrame = workspace.Cords.CFrame
        end
    end
    vnoclip(false)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart.CFrame)
end
function SelectionTp(arg, arg2, arg3)
    OldPosB = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame
    if arg2 == "Sideways" then
        local cFrame = CFrame.new(314.776245, -1.59285617, 87.8067551, 0.999702156, 6.50099711E-4, 0.0243966226, -8.99892871E-9, 0.999645174, -0.0266372953, -0.024405282, 0.0266293604, 0.999347389)
        local v2 = cFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        local cframe = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame
        cframe2 = v2
    elseif arg2 == "Upwards" then
        cframe2 = arg3.CFrame
    end
    camon()
    for _, playerModel in next, game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants() do
        if playerModel:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(playerModel:FindFirstChild("Selection").Parent.CFrame.p) * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
            wait(arg)
            pcall(function()
                if not playerModel.Parent.PrimaryPart then
                    playerModel.Parent.PrimaryPart = playerModel:FindFirstChild("Selection").Parent
                end
                while true do
                    if isnetworkowner(playerModel) then
                        break
                    end
                    game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(playerModel.Parent)
                    task.wait()
                end
                game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(playerModel.Parent)
                playerModel:PivotTo(cframe2)
            end)
            wait(arg)
        end
    end
    camoff()
    delallselections()
    if getgenv2.whendonetp then
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = OldPosB
    end
end
function ClickToTP()
    if ClickTp == false then
        connection2:Disconnect()
        return
    end
    local connection = pos.Button1Up:Connect(function()
        local parent2 = pos.Target.Parent
        if parent2:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if parent2:FindFirstChild("Main") then
                for _, playerModel in pairs(game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
                    if not pos.Target:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                        local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox", parent2.Main)
                        selectionBox.Name = "Selection"
                        selectionBox.Adornee = selectionBox.Parent
                        selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.5
                        selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.09
                        selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                        selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 172, 240)
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif parent2:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if parent2:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                for _, playerModel in pairs(game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
                    if not pos.Target:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                        local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox", parent2.WoodSection)
                        selectionBox.Name = "Selection"
                        selectionBox.Adornee = selectionBox.Parent
                        selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.5
                        selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.09
                        selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                        selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 172, 240)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    connection2 = connection
end
function selectionv2(arg)
    cframe2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
    if getgenv2.selectionv2ON == false then
        Betterselection:Disconnect()
        return
    end
    local connection = pos.Button1Up:Connect(function()
        local flag4
        local v2, v3, v27, v28, v29, v30, v31, v32
        v2, v3, v27, v28, flag4, v29, v30, v31, v32 = game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()
        for _, instance in pairs(v2, v3, v27, v28, flag4, v29, v30, v31, v32) do
            flag4 = false
            local function fn()
                flag4 = true
            end
            local main2 = instance:FindFirstChild("Main")
            do
                local skipped = false
                if not main2 then
                    if not instance:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                        skipped = true
                    end
                end
                if not skipped then
                    if instance:FindFirstChild("Main") then
                        if instance.Main:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                            skipped = true
                        end
                    end
                    if not skipped then
                        if instance:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                            if instance.WoodSection:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
                                skipped = true
                            end
                        end
                        if not skipped then
                            if pos.Target.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                                if tostring(instance.Owner.Value) ~= arg then
                                    fn()
                                end
                                pcall(function()
                                    local main3 = instance:FindFirstChild("Main")
                                    local jump = false
                                    if main3 then
                                        if instance.Main.Parent.Name == pos.Target.Parent.Name then
                                            if not instance:FindFirstChild("DraggableItem") then
                                                if not instance:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName") then
                                                    jump = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                            if not jump then
                                                print(instance)
                                                local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox", instance.Main)
                                                selectionBox.Name = "Selection"
                                                selectionBox.Adornee = selectionBox.Parent
                                                selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.5
                                                selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.09
                                                selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                                selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 172, 240)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    else
                                        jump = true
                                    end
                                    if jump then
                                        if instance:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                                            if instance.TreeClass.Value == pos.Target.Parent.TreeClass.Value then
                                                print("added")
                                                local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox", instance.WoodSection)
                                                selectionBox.Name = "Selection"
                                                selectionBox.Adornee = selectionBox.Parent
                                                selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.5
                                                selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.09
                                                selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                                selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 172, 240)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if flag4 then
                break
            end
        end
    end)
    Betterselection = connection
end
function convertCFrameToTable(arg)
    local tbl3 = {arg:GetComponents()}
    tbl3[1] = tbl3[1] - parent
    tbl3[2] = tbl3[2] - y3
    tbl3[3] = tbl3[3] - z
    return {
        tbl3[1],
        tbl3[2],
        tbl3[3],
        tbl3[4],
        tbl3[5],
        tbl3[6],
        tbl3[7],
        tbl3[8],
        tbl3[9],
        tbl3[10],
        tbl3[11],
        tbl3[12],
    }
end
function CopyBase(arg, arg2)
    list3 = {}
    for _, property in next, game:GetService("Workspace").Properties:GetChildren() do
        if property:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if tostring(property.Owner.Value) == arg2 then
                parent = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.X
                y3 = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.Y
                z = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.Z
            end
        end
    end
    for _, playerModel in ipairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants()) do
        if not string.find(playerModel.Name:lower(), "glass") then
            if playerModel.Name == "BuildDependentWood" then
                if playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("MainCFrame") then
                    if playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("BlueprintWoodClass") then
                        if tostring(playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("Owner").Value) == arg2 then
                            table.insert(list3, {
                                Blueprint = playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("ItemName").Value,
                                WoodClass = playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("BlueprintWoodClass").Value,
                                Position = convertCFrameToTable(playerModel.parent:FindFirstChild("MainCFrame").Value),
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if writefile then
        writefile(tostring(arg .. ".json"), player10:JSONEncode(list3))
    else
        print("Exploit doesnt have writefile")
    end
end
function spawnPart(cframe)
    print(cframe.Blueprint)
    local clientPlacedBlueprint = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").PlaceStructure.ClientPlacedBlueprint
    local blueprint = cframe.Blueprint
    local cFrame = CFrame.new(parent + cframe.Position[1], y3 + cframe.Position[2], z + cframe.Position[3], cframe.Position[4], cframe.Position[5], cframe.Position[6], cframe.Position[7], cframe.Position[8], cframe.Position[9], cframe.Position[10], cframe.Position[11], cframe.Position[12])
    clientPlacedBlueprint:FireServer(blueprint, cFrame, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer)
end
function LoadBaseFile(arg)
    local data
    if readfile then
        data = player10:JSONDecode(readfile(tostring(arg .. ".json")))
    end
    for _, property in next, game:GetService("Workspace").Properties:GetChildren() do
        if property:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if tostring(property.Owner.Value) == game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Name then
                parent = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.X
                y3 = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.Y
                z = property:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare").Position.Z
            end
        end
    end
    wait(1)
    for _, v2 in ipairs(data) do
        if v14 == 50 then
            print("hit 50 fill")
            local nextpage
            repeat
                wait()
                nextpage = getgenv2.nextpage
            until nextpage
            v14 = 0
        end
        v14 = v14 + 1
        spawnPart(v2)
        wait(1)
    end
end
function getAllPlacedBlueprintsInPreviewPositions()
    local list = {}
    local tbl3 = {}
    for _, descendant in pairs(workspace.Preview:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant.Name == "BuildDependentWood" then
            tbl3[descendant.CFrame.Position] = descendant
        end
    end
    for _, playerModel in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants()) do
        if playerModel.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if playerModel.Parent:FindFirstChild("Main") then
                if playerModel.Parent.Main:FindFirstChild("ProgressBillboard") then
                    if playerModel.Parent.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                        if playerModel.Name == "BuildDependentWood" then
                            if playerModel.Parent:FindFirstChild("Type") then
                                if playerModel.Parent.Type.Value == "Blueprint" then
                                    local position = playerModel.CFrame.Position
                                    for k, instance in pairs(tbl3) do
                                        if not instance.Parent.Name ~= playerModel.Parent.Name then
                                            if (position - k).Magnitude <= 4.0E-4 then
                                                table.insert(list, playerModel.Parent)
                                                instance.Parent.Parent = nil
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end
