function SelectionTpWOOD(arg, arg2)
    local findFirstChild, instance8, player2, cframe
    local tbl3 = {}
    local tbl4 = {}
    local v3, v27, v28, v29, v30, v32, v33, v76
    v3, v27, v28, v29, findFirstChild, instance8, player2, cframe, v30, v32, v33, v76 = game.Workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants()
    for _, instance in ipairs(v3, v27, v28, v29, findFirstChild, instance8, player2, cframe, v30, v32, v33, v76) do
        instance8 = instance
        findFirstChild = instance.FindFirstChild
        player2 = "Selection"
        findFirstChild = findFirstChild(instance8, player2)
        if findFirstChild then
            findFirstChild = table
            findFirstChild = findFirstChild.insert
            instance8 = tbl3
            player2 = instance
            findFirstChild(instance8, player2)
        else
            findFirstChild = instance.Name
            if findFirstChild == "Type" then
                findFirstChild = instance.Value
                if findFirstChild == "Blueprint" then
                    findFirstChild = tostring
                    instance8 = instance.Parent
                    instance8 = instance8.Owner
                    instance8 = instance8.Value
                    findFirstChild = findFirstChild(instance8)
                    if findFirstChild == arg2 then
                        findFirstChild = instance.Parent
                        instance8 = findFirstChild
                        findFirstChild = findFirstChild.FindFirstChild
                        player2 = "BuildDependentWood"
                        findFirstChild = findFirstChild(instance8, player2)
                        if findFirstChild then
                            findFirstChild = instance.Parent
                            findFirstChild = findFirstChild.BuildDependentWood
                            findFirstChild = findFirstChild.Transparency
                            if findFirstChild ~= 1 then
                                findFirstChild = table
                                findFirstChild = findFirstChild.insert
                                instance8 = tbl4
                                player2 = instance.Parent
                                player2 = player2.BuildDependentWood
                                findFirstChild(instance8, player2)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for i = 1, math.min(#tbl3, #tbl4) do
        findFirstChild = tbl3[i]
        instance8 = tbl4[i]
        player2 = game
        player2 = player2.Players
        player2 = player2.LocalPlayer
        player2 = player2.Character
        player2 = player2.HumanoidRootPart
        cframe = CFrame
        cframe = cframe.new
        cframe = cframe(findFirstChild:FindFirstChild("Selection").Parent.CFrame.p)
        cframe = cframe * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
        player2.CFrame = cframe
        player2 = task
        player2 = player2.wait
        cframe = arg
        player2(cframe)
        player2 = findFirstChild.Parent
        player2 = player2.PrimaryPart
        if not player2 then
            player2 = findFirstChild.Parent
            local findFirstChild3 = findFirstChild
            cframe = findFirstChild.FindFirstChild
            cframe = cframe(findFirstChild3, "Selection")
            cframe = cframe.Parent
            player2.PrimaryPart = cframe
        end
        player2 = Instance
        player2 = player2.new
        player2 = player2("BodyVelocity", findFirstChild)
        cframe = Instance
        cframe = cframe.new
        cframe = cframe("BodyPosition", findFirstChild)
        player2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        player2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        player2.P = 9000
        cframe.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        cframe.P = 100000
        pcall(function()
            while true do
                if isnetworkowner(findFirstChild.Selection.Parent) then
                    break
                end
                game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(findFirstChild.Parent)
                task.wait(arg)
            end
            game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(findFirstChild.Parent)
            findFirstChild.Selection.Parent:PivotTo(instance8.CFrame)
        end)
        spawn(function()
            wait(5)
            player2:Destroy()
            cframe:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

function updateAutoBuildUI(arg)
    local v2 = #workspace:FindFirstChild("Preview"):GetChildren()
    local placedBPAMT = getgenv2.PlacedBP_AMT
    local autobuildstarttime = getgenv2.AUTOBUILDSTARTTIME
    local num28 = 0
    for _, placeTime in pairs(getgenv2.placeTimes) do
        num28 = num28 + placeTime
    end
    local v3 = math.floor(num28 / #getgenv2.placeTimes) * v2
    arg.MainFrame.time.Text = "Started: " .. tostring(autobuildstarttime) .. "s ago"
    arg.MainFrame.placed.Text = "Placed: " .. tostring(placedBPAMT)
    arg.MainFrame.estimate.Text = "Est: " .. tostring(v3) .. "s left"
    arg.MainFrame.bpAMT.Text = "Bps left: " .. tostring(v2)
end
function v23(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v2 = arg.Size.X * arg.Size.Y * arg.Size.Z
    if arg2 >= v2 and arg3 <= v2 then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

           woodPiece.Parent.PrimaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)\r\n                woodPiece.Parent.PrimaryPart.Anchored = false\r\n            end)\r\n\r\n     loadstring(str14)()

function v24()

        if playerModel.Name == "Selection" then
            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.DestroyStructure:FireServer(playerModel.Parent.Parent)
            wait(1)
        end
    end
end

function things(arg, arg2)
    for _, property in ipairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Properties:GetChildren()) do
        local owner2 = property.Owner
        if owner2 then
            if owner2.Value == nil then
                game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").PropertyPurchasing.ClientPurchasedProperty:FireServer(property, property.OriginSquare.Position)
                game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = property.OriginSquare.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                break
            end
        end
    end
    wait(1.5)
    for _, child in ipairs(instance3:GetChildren()) do
        if child:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
            if child.Owner.Value == game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer then
                if not child:FindFirstChild("Used") then
                    local itemName2 = child.ItemName
                    if itemName2 then
                        itemName2 = child.ItemName.Value
                    end
                    if itemName2 == "PropertySoldSign" then
                        game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(child.Main.CFrame.p) + Vector3.new(0, 0, 2)
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(child, "Take down sold sign")
                        for i = 1, 5 do
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(child)
                            child.Main.CFrame = game:GetService("Players")[arg2].Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                            task.wait(0.2)
                        end
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(child)
                        child.Main.CFrame = game:GetService("Players")[arg2].Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        local boolValue = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                        boolValue.Name = "Used"
                        boolValue.Value = true
                        boolValue.Parent = child
                        local callback19 = coroutine.wrap(function()
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.RequestLoad:InvokeServer(arg, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer)
                        end)
                        callback19()
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function startnewdupe()
    while true do
        if not getgenv2.SoldSignAutoFarm then
            break
        end
        things(getgenv2.bestnummfordupe, getgenv2.Nameforsingdupe)
        local visible
        repeat
            wait()
            visible = v24.Visible
        until visible
        local quit = v24.Quit
        local x = quit.AbsoluteSize.X
        local y = quit.AbsoluteSize.Y
        local x2 = quit.AbsolutePosition.X
        local y2 = quit.AbsolutePosition.Y
        local v2 = x2 + x / 2
        local v3 = y2 + y / 2
        while true do
            if not v24.Visible then
                break
            end
            callback11:SendMouseButtonEvent(v2, v3 + 50, 0, true, game, 0)
            callback11:SendMouseButtonEvent(v2, v3 + 50, 0, false, game, 0)
            wait(0.1)
        end
        wait(1)
    end
end
function Signstuff()
    local num28 = 0
    for _, playerModel in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").PlayerModels:GetDescendants()) do
        if playerModel.Name == "PropertySoldSign" then

        end
    end

        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
end
function BaseDupe()
    if not getconnections then
        str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "getconnections missing! cant dupe",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
        return
    end
    local position = nil
    local result2
    repeat
        task.wait()
        result2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.ClientMayLoad:InvokeServer(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
    until result2
    task.wait()
    task.spawn(function()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.RequestLoad:InvokeServer(slotnumber, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
    end)
    local visible
    repeat
        wait()
        visible = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI:WaitForChild("SelectPurchase").Visible
    until visible == true
    wait(0.5)
    for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase:WaitForChild("Left").MouseButton1Click)) do
        connection.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    wait(0.1)
    for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase:WaitForChild("Left").MouseButton1Click)) do
        connection.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    wait(0.25)
    repeat
        task.wait()
        for _, child in ipairs(game.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Effects"):GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "StructureModel" then
                if child:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare") then
                    local originSquare = child:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
                    if originSquare.Position ~= Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) then
                        position = originSquare.Position
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    until position ~= nil
    wait(0.1)
    for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase:WaitForChild("Purchase").MouseButton1Click)) do
        connection.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    local visible2
    repeat
        task.wait()
        visible2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI:WaitForChild("ConfirmPurchase").Visible
    until visible2 == true
    for _, connection in ipairs(getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.ConfirmPurchase:WaitForChild("Purchase").MouseButton1Click)) do
        connection.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    task.wait(dupewaittime)
    for _, property in ipairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Properties:GetChildren()) do
        if property.OriginSquare.Position ~= position then
            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").PropertyPurchasing.ClientPurchasedProperty:FireServer(property, property.OriginSquare.Position)
            break
        end
    end
    game:Shutdown()
end
function getdupetime()
    local value
    repeat
        wait()
        value = game.Players.LocalPlayer.IsBuyingLand.Value
    until value
    local value2
    repeat
        wait()
        value2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.IsBuyingLand.Value
    until not value2
    local now2 = tick()
    local value3
    repeat
        wait()
        value3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.CurrentlySavingOrLoading.Value
    until not value3
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = "Time elapsed: " .. string.format("%.2f", tick() - now2) .. " seconds",
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
end
function DoesSaveExist(arg)
    local result2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.GetMetaData:InvokeServer(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
    for i = 1, #result2 do
        if i == arg then
            if result2[i].SaveMeta[#result2[i].SaveMeta] then
                return result2[i].SaveMeta[#result2[i].SaveMeta].NumKeys
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
function MaxDupe(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local name, callback20
    if not getconnections then
        str5:message({
            Title = "Butter",
            Description = "getconnections missing! cant dupe",
            Icon = 6023426926,
        })
        return
    end
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = "Started Dupe, Wait!",
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
    local result2
    repeat
        task.wait()
        result2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.ClientMayLoad:InvokeServer(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
    until result2
    task.wait()
    task.spawn(function()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").LoadSaveRequests.RequestLoad:InvokeServer(arg3, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
    end)
    local visible
    repeat
        wait()
        visible = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI:WaitForChild("SelectPurchase").Visible
    until visible == true
    wait(0.5)
    name = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase
    callback20 = "Left"
    local v3, v28, v30, v32, v76, v83
    v3, v28, name, callback20, v30, v32, v76, v83 = getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase.WaitForChild(name, callback20).MouseButton1Click)
    for _, v2 in ipairs(v3, v28, name, callback20, v30, v32, v76, v83) do
        v2.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    wait(0.1)
    name = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase
    callback20 = "Left"
    local v27, v29, v31, v33, v80, v86
    v27, v29, name, callback20, v31, v33, v80, v86 = getconnections(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.PropertyPurchasingGUI.SelectPurchase.WaitForChild(name, callback20).MouseButton1Click)
    for _, v2 in ipairs(v27, v29, name, callback20, v31, v33, v80, v86) do
        v2.Function()
        task.wait()
    end
    wait(0.25)
    if arg2 then
        wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Parent = GetPlot().OriginSquare
            GetPlot().OriginSquare.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        end)
    end
    local children = game:GetService("Workspace").Effects.StructureModel:GetChildren()
    local instance = children[#children - arg]
    name = instance.Name
    callback20 = instance.FindFirstChild
    callback20 = callback20(instance, "Main")
    do
        local skipped = false
        if callback20 then
            callback20 = instance.Main
            callback20 = callback20.Size
            if callback20 then
                skipped = true
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            callback20 = instance.FindFirstChild
            callback20 = callback20(instance, "WoodSection")
            if callback20 then
                callback20 = instance.WoodSection
                callback20 = callback20.Size
                if callback20 then
                    skipped = true
                end
            end
            if not skipped then
                callback20 = instance.FindFirstChild
                callback20 = callback20(instance, "BuildDependentWood")
                if callback20 then
                    callback20 = instance.BuildDependentWood
                    callback20 = callback20.Size
                end
            end
        end
    end
    print("Item to leave on:", name)
    print("Size:", callback20)
    wait(1)
    local connection = game:GetService("Workspace").PlayerModels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:WaitForChild("Owner") then
            if child.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                if child.Name == name then
                    if child:FindFirstChild("Main") then
                        if child.Main.Size == callback20 then
                            game:Shutdown()
                        end
                    elseif child:FindFirstChild("WoodSection") then
                        if child.WoodSection.Size == callback20 then
                            game:Shutdown()
                        end
                    end
                elseif child:FindFirstChild("BuildDependentWood") then
                    if child.BuildDependentWood.Size == callback20 then
                        game:Shutdown()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    str5:message({
        Title = "Mimir",
        Description = "Done Load in a base! NOT ON THE RED LAND!",
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
end

function num23()
    function getItemInfo(arg, arg2)
        for _, v2 in ipairs(list2) do
            if v2.name == arg then
                if v2.type == arg2 then
                    return v2
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    function insertOrUpdateItem(arg, arg2)
        item = getItemInfo(arg, arg2)
        if not item then
            table.insert(list2, {name = arg, amount = 1, type = arg2})
        else
            item.amount = item.amount + 1
        end
    end
    function processPlayerModel(instance)
        main = instance:FindFirstChild("Main")
        owner = instance:FindFirstChild("Owner")
        local itemName2 = instance:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
        if not itemName2 then
            itemName2 = instance:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName")
            if not itemName2 then
                itemName2 = instance:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
            end
        end
        itemName = itemName2
        if main then
            if owner then
                if owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                    insertOrUpdateItem(itemName.Value, itemName.Value)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, playerModel in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        processPlayerModel(playerModel)
    end
    for _, v2 in pairs(list2) do
        print(v2.name .. " : " .. tostring(v2.amount) .. " as: " .. v2.type)
    end
end

function v25()
    num23 = {}
    print("\n----Items / Structures----\n")
    for _, playerModel in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        local itemName2 = playerModel:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
        if not itemName2 then
            itemName2 = playerModel:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName")
            if not itemName2 then
                itemName2 = playerModel:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
            end
        end
        if itemName2 then
            local value = itemName2.Value
            local v2 = num23
            local v3 = num23[value]
            do
                local skipped = false
                if v3 then
                    v3 = num23[value] + 1
                    if v3 then
                        skipped = true
                    end
                end
                if not skipped then
                    v3 = 1
                end
            end
            v2[value] = v3
        end
    end
    for k, v2 in num23, nil, nil do
        print(k, v2)
    end
    num23 = {}
    print("\n----Wood----\n")
    for _, playerModel in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()) do
        local treeClass = playerModel:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
        if treeClass then
            local value = treeClass.Value
            local v2 = num23
            local v3 = num23[value]
            do
                local skipped = false
                if v3 then
                    v3 = num23[value] + 1
                    if v3 then
                        skipped = true
                    end
                end
                if not skipped then
                    v3 = 1
                end
            end
            v2[value] = v3
        end
    end

function ModWood()
    SelectSawmill("Mod Wood")
    local ModWoodSawmill2
    repeat
        wait()
        ModWoodSawmill2 = ModWoodSawmill
    until ModWoodSawmill2
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = "Click a tree",
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
    num26 = true
    if not num26 then
        return
    end
    local connection = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse().Button1Down:Connect(function()
        local target2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse().Target
        if target2 then
            if target2.Parent then
                if target2.Parent:FindFirstAncestor("LogModels") then
                    if target2.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") then
                        if target2.Parent.Owner.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                            for _, descendant in pairs(target2.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                                if descendant.Name == "ChildIDs" then
                                    if #descendant:GetChildren() == 0 then
                                        table.insert(list5, descendant.Parent.ID.Value)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            table.sort(list5)
                            for _, descendant in pairs(target2.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                                if descendant.Name == "ChildIDs" then
                                    for _, child in pairs(descendant:GetChildren()) do
                                        if child.Value == list5[#list5] then
                                            parentbranch = child.Parent.Parent
                                            Instance.new("Highlight", parentbranch)
                                        end
                                    end
                                elseif descendant.Name == "ID" then
                                    if descendant.Value == list5[#list5] then
                                        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight", descendant.Parent)
                                        highlight.FillColor = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                                        childbranchId = list5[#list5]
                                        childbranch = descendant.Parent
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    connection7 = connection
    local childbranch2
    repeat
        wait()
        childbranch2 = childbranch
    until childbranch2
    local lava = GetLava()
    lava.Lava.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    firstpart = childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = firstpart.CFrame
    wait(0.2)
    local lavaFire
    repeat
        wait()
        while true do
            if isnetworkowner(parentbranch) then
                break
            end
            game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(parentbranch.Parent)
            task.wait()
        end
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(parentbranch.Parent)
        parentbranch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(-1425, 489, 1244))
        if identifyexecutor() == "Wave" then
            firetouchinterest(parentbranch, lava.Lava, true)
            firetouchinterest(parentbranch, lava.Lava, false)
        else
            firetouchinterest(parentbranch, lava.Lava, 0)
            firetouchinterest(parentbranch, lava.Lava, 1)
        end
        lavaFire = parentbranch:FindFirstChild("LavaFire")
    until lavaFire
    ResizeBar(1, 3)
    firstpart = childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = firstpart.CFrame
    wait(0.3)
    while true do
        if isnetworkowner(firstpart) then
            break
        end
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(firstpart.Parent)
        firstpart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        childbranch.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        task.wait()
    end
    game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(firstpart.Parent)
    firstpart:PivotTo(CFrame.new(-1055, 291, -458))
    wait(0.3)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = childbranch.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
    while true do
        if isnetworkowner(childbranch) then
            break
        end
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(childbranch.Parent)
        task.wait()
    end
    game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(childbranch.Parent)
    childbranch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(-1055, 291, -458))
    parentbranch:FindFirstChild("LavaFire"):Destroy()
    parentbranch:FindFirstChild("BodyAngularVelocity"):Destroy()
    parentbranch:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity"):Destroy()
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = parentbranch.CFrame
    wait(0.1)
    local parent2
    repeat
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(parentbranch.Parent)
        parentbranch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(314.54, -0.5, 86.823))
        task.wait()
        parent2 = parentbranch.Parent
    until not parent2
    ResizeBar(2, 3)
    firstpart = childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
    ifworked()
    task.spawn(function()
        local worked2
        repeat
            wait()
            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = firstpart.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
            worked2 = getgenv2.worked
        until worked2
    end)
    local v2 = isSolara()
    if v2 then
        v2 = getBestAxeNoRequire()
    end
    local bestAxe, axeDamage
    if not v2 then
        bestAxe = GetBestAxe(childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("TreeClass").Value)
        axeDamage = GetAxeDamage(childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("TreeClass").Value)
    else
        bestAxe = v2[1]
        axeDamage = v2[3]
    end
    local worked
    repeat
        wait()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Interaction.RemoteProxy:FireServer(childbranch.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent"), {
            tool = bestAxe,
            faceVector = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
            height = 0.3,
            sectionId = 1,
            hitPoints = axeDamage,
            cooldown = 0.25837870788574,
            cuttingClass = "Axe",
        })
        worked = getgenv2.worked
    until worked
    task.wait(0.3)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = childbranch.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
    while true do
        if isnetworkowner(childbranch) then
            break
        end
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = childbranch.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(childbranch.Parent)
        task.wait()
    end
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = childbranch.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
    task.wait(0.2)
    game.ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging:FireServer(childbranch.Parent)
    task.wait(0.3)
    childbranch:PivotTo(ModWoodSawmill.Particles.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
    ResizeBar(3, 3)
    str5:message({Title = "Butter", Description = "Done", Icon = 6023426926})
    num26 = false
    childbranch = nil
    parentbranch = nil
    getgenv2.worked = false
    list5 = {}
    firstpart = nil
    ModWoodSawmill = nil
    connection8:Disconnect()
    connection7:Disconnect()
end

camSprintKey = Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift
localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
callback9 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
callback8 = UserInputService
movePosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
moveDirection = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
targetMovePosition = movePosition
Y_Sensitivity = 300
X_Sensitivity = 300
lastRightButtonDown = Vector2.new(0, 0)
rightMouseButtonDown = false
targetFOV = 70
sprinting = false
sprintingSpeed = 3
keysDown = {}
local tbl8 = {}
tbl8[Enum.KeyCode.D] = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
tbl8[Enum.KeyCode.A] = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)
tbl8[Enum.KeyCode.S] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
tbl8[Enum.KeyCode.W] = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
moveKeys = tbl8
function Tweenn(arg, arg2, arg3)
    if arg3 == 1 then
        return arg2
    end
    if tonumber(arg) then
        return arg * (1 - arg3) + arg2 * arg3
    end
    return arg:Lerp(arg2, arg3)
end
function ClampVector3(pos2, pos3, pos4)
    return Vector3.new(math.clamp(pos2.X, pos3.X, pos4.X), math.clamp(pos2.Y, pos3.Y, pos4.Y), math.clamp(pos2.Z, pos3.Z, pos4.Z))
end
callback8.InputChanged:connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        movePosition = movePosition + Vector2.new(input.Delta.x, input.Delta.y)
    end
end)
function CalculateMovement()
    newMovement = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    for k, v2 in pairs(keysDown) do
        local newMovement2 = newMovement
        local v3 = moveKeys[k]
        if not v3 then
            v3 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        newMovement = newMovement2 + v3
    end
    return newMovement
end
function Round(arg, arg2)
    return math.floor(arg / arg2 + 0.5) * arg2
end
function Input(arg, arg2)
    if moveKeys[arg.KeyCode] then
        if arg.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
            keysDown[arg.KeyCode] = true
        elseif arg.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
            keysDown[arg.KeyCode] = nil
        end
    elseif arg.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
        if arg.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
            rightMouseButtonDown = true
            lastRightButtonDown = Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y)
            callback8.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
        elseif arg.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
            targetFOV = 20
        elseif arg.KeyCode == camSprintKey then
            sprinting = true
        end
    elseif arg.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
        rightMouseButtonDown = false
        callback8.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
    elseif arg.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
        targetFOV = 70
    elseif arg.KeyCode == camSprintKey then
        sprinting = false
    end
end
pos.WheelForward:connect(function()
    callback9.CoordinateFrame = callback9.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
end)
pos.WheelBackward:connect(function()
    callback9.CoordinateFrame = callback9.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(-0, -0, 5)
end)
callback8.InputBegan:connect(Input)
callback8.InputEnded:connect(Input)
function camon()
    Toggled = true
    callback9.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
end
function camoff()
    Toggled = false
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
    game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid
    game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = "Custom"
end
game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if Toggled then
        mouseTarget = pos.Hit
        targetMovePosition = movePosition
        local v2 = callback9
        local v3 = CFrame.new(callback9.CoordinateFrame.p) * CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(-targetMovePosition.Y / Y_Sensitivity, -targetMovePosition.X / X_Sensitivity, 0)
        local v27 = CalculateMovement()
        local tbl3 = {}
        tbl3[true] = sprintingSpeed
        local v28 = tbl3[sprinting]
        if not v28 then
            v28 = 0.5
        end
        v2.CoordinateFrame = v3 * CFrame.new(v27 * v28)
        callback9.FieldOfView = Tweenn(callback9.FieldOfView, targetFOV, 0.5)
        if rightMouseButtonDown then
            callback8.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
            movePosition = movePosition - (lastRightButtonDown - Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y))
            lastRightButtonDown = Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y)
        end
    end
end)
function fixnames()
    for _, playerModel in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").PlayerModels:GetDescendants()) do
        if playerModel.Name == "Model" then
            if playerModel:FindFirstChild("ItemName") then
                playerModel.Name = playerModel.ItemName.Value
            end
        end
    end
end

                theG2 = theG2(v27, readfile2, str13, v29, v30, v31, v32, v33, v76, v80, v83, v86, v89, v92, v95, v98, v101, v103, v106, v108, v110, v113, v116, v119, v121, v123, v125, v127, v130, v133, v136)
                v3 = theG2
            end
            theG2 = tostring
            theG2 = theG2(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
            v3[theG2] = tbl4
            theG2 = writefile
            if theG2 then
                theG2 = writefile

                theG2("ButterSSlotNames.cfg", readfile2, str13, v27, v29, v30, v31, v32, v33, v76, v80, v83, v86, v89, v92, v95, v98, v101, v103, v106, v108, v110, v113, v116, v119, v121, v123, v125, v127, v130, v133)
            else
                theG2 = print
                theG2("Writefile not in your exploit!")
            end
        end
        if readfile then
            theG2 = player10
            local jSONDecode = player10.JSONDecode
            readfile2 = "ButterSSlotNames.cfg"
            local v29, v30, v31, v32, v33, v76, v80, v83, v86, v89, v92, v95, v98, v101, v103, v106, v108, v110, v113, v116, v119, v121, v123, v125, v127, v130, v133, v136, v138
            v29, readfile2, str13, v30, v31, v32, v33, v76, v80, v83, v86, v89, v92, v95, v98, v101, v103, v106, v108, v110, v113, v116, v119, v121, v123, v125, v127, v130, v133, v136, v138 = readfile(readfile2)
            local v27 = jSONDecode(theG2, v29, readfile2, str13, v30, v31, v32, v33, v76, v80, v83, v86, v89, v92, v95, v98, v101, v103, v106, v108, v110, v113, v116, v119, v121, v123, v125, v127, v130, v133, v136, v138)
        end
    end
else
    print("No writefile")
end
function Dragger()
    game.Workspace.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "Dragger" then
            child.Color = Color3.fromRGB(theR, theG, theB)
            local bodyGyro = child:WaitForChild("BodyGyro")
            local bodyPosition = child:WaitForChild("BodyPosition")
            repeat
                if flag3 then
                    task.wait()
                    bodyPosition.P = 120000
                    bodyPosition.D = 1000
                    bodyPosition.maxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                    bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                else
                    wait()
                    bodyPosition.P = 10000
                    bodyPosition.D = 800
                    bodyPosition.maxForce = Vector3.new(17000, 17000, 17000)
                    bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(200, 200, 200)
                end
            until not child
        end
    end)
end

Home2:Button("Server Hop", function()
    -- inlined from the old remote ServerHop.txt (cleaned: guarded file IO, no endless double-fetch)
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    local hour = os.date("!*t").hour
    local fileName = "NotSameServers.json"
    local visited = {hour}
    if readfile and isfile and isfile(fileName) then
        local ok, data = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(fileName))
        end)
        if ok and type(data) == "table" and data[1] == hour then
            visited = data
        end
    end
    local cursor = ""
    for _ = 1, 60 do
        local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
        if cursor ~= "" then
            url = url .. "&cursor=" .. cursor
        end
        local ok, site = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
        end)
        if ok and type(site) == "table" and site.data then
            for _, server in ipairs(site.data) do
                local id = tostring(server.id)
                if tonumber(server.maxPlayers) > tonumber(server.playing) and id ~= game.JobId and not table.find(visited, id) then
                    table.insert(visited, id)
                    if writefile then
                        pcall(writefile, fileName, HttpService:JSONEncode(visited))
                    end
                    pcall(function()
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, id, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
                    end)
                    task.wait(4)
                end
            end
            cursor = site.nextPageCursor or ""
        else
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end
end)

local UserInputService2 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
function beginSprint(arg, arg2)
    if not arg2 then
        if arg.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            if arg.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then
                if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ItemDraggingGUI.CanRotate.Visible then
                    getgenv2.Speed = getgenv2.SprintSpeed
                    Walkspeed()
                else
                    getgenv2.Speed = 0
                    Walkspeed()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function endSprint(arg, arg2)
    if not arg2 then
        if arg.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            if arg.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then
                getgenv2.Speed = Nspeed
                Walkspeed()
            end
        end
    end
end
UserInputService2.InputBegan:Connect(beginSprint)
UserInputService2.InputEnded:Connect(endSprint)

game.Lighting.Changed:Connect(function()
    if AlwaysDay then
        if AlwaysNight then
            return
        end
        game.Lighting.TimeOfDay = "12:00:00"
        game.Lighting.SunPos.Value = 1
        local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local Lighting2 = game:GetService("Lighting")
        Lighting2.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local Lighting3 = game:GetService("Lighting")
        Lighting3.ColorShift_Top = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    end
    if AlwaysNight then
        if AlwaysDay then
            return
        end
        game.Lighting.TimeOfDay = "00:00:00"
        game.Lighting.SunPos.Value = -1
    end
    if NoFog then
        game.Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
    end
end)

local WorldTab12 = WorldTab2
function theG2()
    for _, part in next, game.Workspace:GetDescendants() do
        if part:IsA("Part") then
            if part.Name == "SeaSand" then
                part.Size = Vector3.new(2048, 60, 2048)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, part in next, game.Workspace:GetDescendants() do
        if part:IsA("Part") then
            if part.Name == "Water" then
                part.Size = Vector3.new(20480, 6, 20080)
                game.Workspace.Terrain:fillBlock(part.CFrame, part.Size, Enum.Material.Water)
                part:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end

    do
        local skipped = false

            autobuildPing = GetAutobuildPing()

    end
    getgenv4.autobuildWait = autobuildPing
end

function readfile2(arg)
    if arg == true then
        player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        humanoid = player.Character.Humanoid
        if humanoid.Seated then
            local seatPart = player.Character.Humanoid.SeatPart
            if seatPart then
                if seatPart.Parent.Type.Value == "Vehicle" then
                    NOFLY()
                    wait()
                    sFLY(true)
                end
            end
        end
    else
        NOFLY()
    end
end

function readfile2()
    local v2 = isSolara()
    if v2 or not require then
        getgenv2.treestop = false
        treeCutNoRequire(getTree, treeCutAmt)
        print("No req")
        return
    end
    BringTreetime = tick()
    if getTree == "LoneCave" then
        bringTree(getTree, true)
    else
        for i = 1, treeCutAmt do
            wait()
            bringTree(getTree, false)
            ResizeBar(i, treeCutAmt)
        end
    end
    str5:message({
        Title = "Butter",
        Description = "Done in " .. string.format("%.1fs", tick() - BringTreetime),
        Icon = 6023426926,
    })
    if getTree == "LoneCave" then
        GetToolsfix()
    end
end
button4(theG2, "Bring Tree", readfile2)

function SaveSlotNames()
    print("saved")
    do
        local skipped = false
        if writefile then
            if isfile then
                skipped = true
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            print("Missing writefile")
            return
        end
    end
    if isfile("ButterSSlotNames.cfg") then
        local tbl3 = {}
        local str14 = tostring(theG2)
        tbl3[str14] = {
            Slot1Val = getgenv2.SSlot1,
            Slot2Val = getgenv2.SSlot2,
            Slot3Val = getgenv2.SSlot3,
            Slot4Val = getgenv2.SSlot4,
            Slot5Val = getgenv2.SSlot5,
            Slot6Val = getgenv2.SSlot6,
        }
        writefile("ButterSSlotNames.cfg", player10:JSONEncode(tbl3))
    end
end
function readfile2()
    local getgenv3 = getgenv
    do
        local skipped = false
        if getgenv3 then
            getgenv3 = getgenv()
            if getgenv3 then
                skipped = true
            end
        end
        if not skipped then
            getgenv3 = _G
            if not getgenv3 then
                getgenv3 = getfenv(0)
                if not getgenv3 then
                    getgenv3 = {}
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, cframe in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.LoadSaveGUI.SlotList.Main:GetChildren()) do
        local name = cframe.Name
        if name == "SlotItem" or cframe:FindFirstChild("SlotName") then
            if cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 60) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot1
            elseif cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 120) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot2
            elseif cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 180) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot3
            elseif cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 240) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot4
            elseif cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 300) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot5
            elseif cframe.Position == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 360) then
                cframe.SlotName.Text = getgenv3.SSlot6
            end
        end
    end
end

local v286 = str13
str13 = str13.Destroy
str13(v286)
str13 = {}
function flyDetect(player2)
    task.spawn(function()
        local character = player2.Character
        if not character then
            character = player2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        end
        local humanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local num28 = 0
        local now2 = tick()
        while true do
            if not str13[player2.Name] then
                if getgenv2.flydtc then
                    local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
                    local v2 = -Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                    local num29 = 1000
                    local v3 = RaycastParams.new()
                    v3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
                    v3.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
                    local raycastResult = Workspace:Raycast(position, v2 * num29, v3)
                    local distance = raycastResult and raycastResult.Distance or num29
                    if 15 < distance then
                        num28 = num28 + (tick() - now2)
                    else
                        num28 = 0
                        now2 = tick()
                    end
                    if 5 <= num28 then
                        str5:message({
                            Title = "Exploiter detected",
                            Description = player2.Name .. " is flying | " .. tostring(math.floor(distance)) .. " studs up",
                            Icon = 6023426926,
                            Length = 10,
                        })
                        num28 = 0
                        now2 = tick()
                        str13[player2.Name] = true
                        task.spawn(function()
                            task.wait(10)
                            str13[player2.Name] = nil
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end
for _, player2 in ipairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
    if player2.Name ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name then
        flyDetect(player2)
    end
end
game.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player2)
    flyDetect(player2)
end)
